# Authored skill-slot specifications

Design drafts for all five forms. These describe what must be authored and verified, not currently verified student screens.

## number-0

### number-0-01 — AC9MFN01

Connect spoken zero and a nonzero number name to their numerals.

- Response: selection; thinking: understanding; expected difficulty: accessible (uncalibrated).
- Required representation: Numeral cards including zero; neutral spoken prompt.
- Keep equivalent: Include zero and one matched nonzero target in every form; same range to 20 and choices. Same instruction length, interaction, read-aloud access and amount of supplied information across all five forms.
- Scoring: Selected numeral matches the requested number.
- Calculator: not-provided. Provide the construction, measurement or data tools specified in the slot; identical controls across all five forms.

### number-0-02 — AC9MFN02

Recognise a small quantity without counting each object.

- Response: number; thinking: understanding; expected difficulty: accessible (uncalibrated).
- Required representation: Briefly present a dot collection up to five; accessible viewing/replay policy must be identical across forms.
- Keep equivalent: Same quantity band and exposure conditions; do not infer subitising from accuracy alone. Same instruction length, interaction, read-aloud access and amount of supplied information across all five forms.
- Scoring: Correct quantity; method evidence remains limited without observation.
- Calculator: not-provided. Provide the construction, measurement or data tools specified in the slot; identical controls across all five forms.

### number-0-03 — AC9MFN03

Count a collection of 20.

- Response: number; thinking: understanding; expected difficulty: accessible (uncalibrated).
- Required representation: Separate countable objects with no overlapping or hidden members.
- Keep equivalent: Include 20 in every form; same spacing complexity and object size. Same instruction length, interaction, read-aloud access and amount of supplied information across all five forms.
- Scoring: Exact total; no numeral label supplying the answer.
- Calculator: not-provided. Provide the construction, measurement or data tools specified in the slot; identical controls across all five forms.

### number-0-04 — AC9MFN04

Combine two visible parts into a whole up to ten.

- Response: number; thinking: understanding; expected difficulty: accessible (uncalibrated).
- Required representation: Two collections in a part-part-whole layout; whole is blank.
- Keep equivalent: Same total band and part sizes. Same instruction length, interaction, read-aloud access and amount of supplied information across all five forms.
- Scoring: Exact whole quantity.
- Calculator: not-provided. Provide the construction, measurement or data tools specified in the slot; identical controls across all five forms.

### number-0-05 — AC9MFN05

Represent adding to a collection.

- Response: construction; thinking: application; expected difficulty: moderate (uncalibrated).
- Required representation: Show a short add-to scene and movable objects; do not pre-build the final collection.
- Keep equivalent: Initial collection and added amount remain within ten; same action structure. Same instruction length, interaction, read-aloud access and amount of supplied information across all five forms.
- Scoring: Submitted model represents both groups and the correct resulting quantity.
- Calculator: not-provided. Provide the construction, measurement or data tools specified in the slot; identical controls across all five forms.

### number-0-06 — AC9MFN06

Share a small collection equally.

- Response: construction; thinking: application; expected difficulty: moderate (uncalibrated).
- Required representation: Movable objects and equally sized recipient areas.
- Keep equivalent: Same number of recipients and divisibility; no remainder. Same instruction length, interaction, read-aloud access and amount of supplied information across all five forms.
- Scoring: All objects allocated equally with none lost or duplicated.
- Calculator: not-provided. Provide the construction, measurement or data tools specified in the slot; identical controls across all five forms.

### number-0-07 — AC9MFA01

Copy a repeating pattern.

- Response: construction; thinking: application; expected difficulty: accessible (uncalibrated).
- Required representation: Show the complete repeated unit and a separate blank construction row.
- Keep equivalent: Same unit length, repetitions and number of attributes. Same instruction length, interaction, read-aloud access and amount of supplied information across all five forms.
- Scoring: Constructed row repeats the source unit in the correct order.
- Calculator: not-provided. Provide the construction, measurement or data tools specified in the slot; identical controls across all five forms.

### number-0-08 — AC9MFN01

Order numerals including zero and 20.

- Response: ordering; thinking: application; expected difficulty: accessible (uncalibrated).
- Required representation: Movable numeral cards; no pre-sorted track revealing order.
- Keep equivalent: Same number of cards and comparable gaps; include zero and 20 in every form. Same instruction length, interaction, read-aloud access and amount of supplied information across all five forms.
- Scoring: All cards ordered correctly.
- Calculator: not-provided. Provide the construction, measurement or data tools specified in the slot; identical controls across all five forms.

### number-0-09 — AC9MFN04

Find a missing part of a whole up to ten.

- Response: number; thinking: understanding; expected difficulty: moderate (uncalibrated).
- Required representation: Whole and one part represented with materials; other part blank.
- Keep equivalent: Same whole/part complexity; do not hide essential data. Same instruction length, interaction, read-aloud access and amount of supplied information across all five forms.
- Scoring: Exact missing part.
- Calculator: not-provided. Provide the construction, measurement or data tools specified in the slot; identical controls across all five forms.

### number-0-10 — AC9MFN03

Compare two collections and demonstrate why one has more.

- Response: construction; thinking: application; expected difficulty: challenging (uncalibrated).

- Keep equivalent: Same difference and count range. Same instruction length, interaction, read-aloud access and amount of supplied information across all five forms.
- Scoring: Correct comparison plus valid pairing/count evidence.
- Calculator: not-provided. Provide the construction, measurement or data tools specified in the slot; identical controls across all five forms.

Represent taking away from a collection.

- Response: construction; thinking: application; expected difficulty: moderate (uncalibrated).

- Calculator: not-provided. Provide the construction, measurement or data tools specified in the slot; identical controls across all five forms.

### number-0-12 — AC9MFN04

Partition a whole into two parts.

- Response: construction; thinking: application; expected difficulty: moderate (uncalibrated).

- Calculator: not-provided. Provide the construction, measurement or data tools specified in the slot; identical controls across all five forms.

## number-1

### number-1-01 — AC9M1N01

Represent a number with tens and ones.

- Response: construction; thinking: understanding; expected difficulty: accessible (uncalibrated).
- Required representation: Provide movable place-value units and a blank model; distinguish hundreds/tens/ones and fractional units without pre-filling the answer.
- Keep equivalent: Range to 120; same number of tens and presence of zero ones. Keep the stated subskill, response mechanism, scoring, reading support and mathematical workload equivalent across all five forms; author different examples.
- Scoring: Submitted units represent the requested value and any specified partition; conserve value and accept all valid configurations.
- Calculator: not-provided. Provide the construction, measurement or data tools specified in the slot; identical controls across all five forms.

### number-1-02 — AC9M1N01

Order numbers spanning the hundred boundary.

- Response: ordering; thinking: application; expected difficulty: moderate (uncalibrated).
- Required representation: Provide unsorted labelled cards and an empty ordered row; audio must read the values without sorting them.
- Keep equivalent: Four cards: one value from 95–99, plus 100, 110 and 120; same zero placeholders and boundary crossings, comparable rather than identical gaps. Keep the stated subskill, response mechanism, scoring, reading support and mathematical workload equivalent across all five forms; author different examples.
- Scoring: Every card must be ordered correctly; equivalent values and ties need an explicit authored policy.
- Calculator: not-provided. Provide the construction, measurement or data tools specified in the slot; identical controls across all five forms.

### number-1-03 — AC9M1N01

Locate and name numbers up to 120.

- Keep equivalent: Same interval and labelled endpoints; include the 120 boundary in the form. Keep the stated subskill, response mechanism, scoring, reading support and mathematical workload equivalent across all five forms; author different examples.
- Scoring: Correct location/value at the declared line resolution; save target position and scale.
- Calculator: not-provided. Provide the construction, measurement or data tools specified in the slot; identical controls across all five forms.

Partition a two-digit number into tens and ones.

- Calculator: not-provided. Provide the construction, measurement or data tools specified in the slot; identical controls across all five forms.

### number-1-05 — AC9M1N02

Create different partitions of one- and two-digit numbers.

- Calculator: not-provided. Provide the construction, measurement or data tools specified in the slot; identical controls across all five forms.

### number-1-06 — AC9M1N03

Quantify a collection grouped in tens.

- Response: number; thinking: application; expected difficulty: accessible (uncalibrated).

### number-1-07 — AC9M1N03

Group a collection in fives to count efficiently.
